   - Block coordinate / local QP repair on top active bins.
   - Simulated annealing / frozen active bins: choose random subset of high bins and random subset of variables, solve a small quadratic program to reduce max while maintaining sum and nonneg.

Now optimization algorithms in detail.

Normalization: Work with u (scaled) length n, sum_target = sqrt(n). Objective internal obj = 2 * max(y) / (sum_u^2 / n?) Wait if we don't enforce sum exactly? Let's define objective for arbitrary u: obj = 2*n*max(y)/sum_u^2. Homogeneous. During optimization, we can fix sum_u = sqrt(n) to simplify. Gradient of obj = constant factor if sum fixed. After each step, renormalize to sum_target. Then internal obj = 2*n*max_y / S^2 = 2*max_y / (S^2/n) if S=√n => 2*max_y. We can track both.
For parent a with sum maybe? We need convert to u: if parent list p_sum maybe, u = parent * sqrt(n) / sum(parent) if len = n. Then sum=√n.
General helper normalize_u(a, n): s=sum; a=s*a/s_target? Actually s_target=np.sqrt(n). a = a*s_target/s. Clip nonneg.

Smooth LSE objective: J = logsumexp(beta*y) perhaps. Since y≥0. We can compute c=y (full conv length 2n-1). To avoid overflow, subtract max. Use softmax weights p = exp(beta y - beta max_y)/sum. Derivative of smooth objective wrt y is p. But objective we actually care max_y, not logsum. For gradient descent on F_smooth = logsumexp(beta y)/beta? Gradient = p (since d/d y = softmax). Scaling β doesn't matter for direction if update normalized. We can use p=softmax(beta y). Then gradient g=2*corr(p,a). Then we need constrained step preserving sum: subtract weighted? Previous subtract mean of g. Since we want move to reduce y with sum fixed. Use g_centered = g - (sum_u * mean? Wait constraint sum u=U. The Lagrange multiplier for projection: if we take step δ = -η g, and require ∑δ=0, subtract average of δ (since all weights equal): δ= -η(g - mean(g)). Then project nonneg and renormalize. That's what previous did. Good. But scale g by max or norm. Use adaptive learning rate: try step, accept if max_y decreases; else backtracking. This is robust.
Pseudo _smooth_step(u,beta): compute y, max; p=softmax(beta y); grad=2*corr(p,u); grad -= mean(grad); mag=||grad||_inf; step = lr*grad/mag; for attempts lr in [eta, eta/2,...], v=u - step; v=max(v,0); if sum>0 rescale to U; compute new max; if new_max < old_max - tol accept. This avoids evaluating full objective denominator. We want minimize max_y, sum fixed. Use beta adapt: start β = n / (max_y - mean?) or β ~ 1/(std) perhaps. Previous τ for a sum n correspond y? We can set beta = 20 / spread? For sparse active, high β. We can anneal from low to high. For y values around τ~0.75, max-min spread maybe 0.5. beta 5-50. Start β=5, increase each accepted? Or anneal beta from 1/n? Let's think. LSE with β large approximates max. If β too small, gradient uniform and slow. We can set β = 10 / (max_y - mean_y +1e-12) but cap. For n large, bandwidth ~? y average 0.5, max0.75, spread0.25, beta40 gives top weights. Use beta schedule: start 2, max 50? For n=3809, maybe beta 10-30. We can adapt: if progress standard.
However earlier objective with sum n used y? If previous smooth used softmax over c where c≈ n*Y? Maybe β different. We'll implement general beta schedule parameterized by n: beta = max(1.0, min(200.0, 20.0 / (y.max() - y.mean() + 1e-9))). This yields ~80 if spread0.25. Could be too high causing non-smooth but okay. Maybe cap 50.

Exact active-set direction: Instead of subgradient of one max, we can take top-K bins with y near max. Let p = softmax over top? Or average gradients of top m where y≥max-δ. This reduces multiple active constraints. Compute p_k = exp(beta*(y_k - max)) with β high, but only top? We can do hard_multi: identify active bins y_k >= max - eps*max (e.g., eps=0.02). For each active k, compute derivative vector d_k (2 shifted u). Sum them with weights (y_k-thresh) and gradient descent. Computing each full d length O(K n). For K up to maybe 100, n=3809 ->380k per iteration, okay. But we can approximate by using softmax p over all bins with high beta; gradient via FFT already. So smooth low beta? Actually high beta p gives multi-active.

Adaptive step with line search: For smooth, we can compute new_y via FFT each attempt; expensive. Use armijo-like? We can try one step with lr ~0.05-0.5 and check. For max objective, Lyapunov? Could just do fixed small. Use backtracking up to 3 attempts.
Learning rate: Previous used normalized gradient g/mg and eta 0.04. Since u values ~1, step small. We can start lr=0.05, increase if no decrease? Use adaptive.

Hard active repair: Pick the maximum bin k*. Derivative d (length n) = 2 * u shifted. Do projected step. But repeated single max can zigzag. We can choose k with largest y - (mean target) and use line search. Could also apply "pair swapping": For active bin k with y too high, decrease overlap between high pairs and increase elsewhere. The d vector tells exactly increasing u_j decreases y_k proportional to neighbor u_{k-j}. We can perform a small redistribution: take mass from indices with large d_j to indices with small d_j (where derivative small), preserving sum. This is like moving along -d centered. Pseudo:
def _smooth_refine(u,n,end_time,tag,start_beta=1.0,end_beta=50.0,eta=0.05,it_limit=100000):
    u = _normalize(u,n)
    best_u=u.copy(); best_obj=_obj(u,n)
    beta=start_beta
    stall=0
    good=0
    t0=time.monotonic()
    for it in range(it_limit):
       if time.monotonic()>=end_time: break
       y=_conv_fft(u,n); max_y=float(y.max())
       obj=_obj_from_max_y(max_y,n) # if sum fixed 2*max_y; but maybe sum not? use formula
       if obj < best_obj -1e-12: update, stall=0, good+=1; print every 500
       else: stall+=1
       spread = max_y - float(y.mean()) ; if spread<1e-12: break
       # beta anneal: increase over time
       progress = min(1.0, (time.monotonic()-t0)/max(1.0,total_budget?)) maybe pass beta as function? Simpler: beta = max(beta*(1+1e-4), min(end_beta, beta+0.05)).
       p = _softmax_y(y,beta)
       g = _grad_fft(p,u,n)
       g -= g.mean()
       mg=np.max(np.abs(g)); if mg<1e-15 break
       # adaptive lr: try eta, then halve
       accepted=False
       for lr in (eta, 0.5*eta, 0.25*eta, 0.1*eta):
           v = u - lr*g/mg
           v=np.maximum(v,0.0)
           v=_normalize(v,n)
           if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)): continue
           yv=_conv_fft(v,n); maxv=float(yv.max()); objv=_obj_from_max(maxv,n)
           if objv < obj - 1e-12: # accept
               u=v.copy(); obj=objv; accepted=True; break
       if not accepted:
           # maybe take small positive step? no. eta = max(eta*0.7,1e-6); stall increase
       if stall > 500:
           eta=max(eta*0.8,1e-6)
           # occasionally randomize beta or jitter
           if stall>2000: add small jitter? u = normalize(u + rng.normal? but rng global? Pass rng)
           stall=0
    return best_u,best_obj

def _lp_direction(u,n,M):
    if n>2000: return None (maybe). A rows = 2n-1, cols=n.
    data=[]; row_ind=[]; col_ind=[]
    for k in range(2n-1):
       lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
       for i in lo..hi: data.append(u[i]); row_ind.append(k); col_ind.append(i)
    A=coo_matrix((data,(row_ind,col_ind)), shape=(2n-1,n)).tocsc()
    b=np.full(rows, M)
    res=linprog(c=-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0, B), method='highs', options={'presolve':True})
    if success: v=res.x; # maybe ensure constraints
    if sum(v)>=U: rescale? For direction, return v normalized? We'll mix.

Helper: _next_fft_len(n): return 1 << (2*n-2).bit_length(). For n=1, length=1? 2n-2=0, bit_length 0 ->1<<0=1. Good.

Convolution FFT:
def _conv_fft(a, n, L=None):
    if L is None: L=_next_fft_len(n)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, L)
    return c[:2*n-1]
For real, A*A complex; product may have small imaginary after irfft.

Softmax weights:
def _softmax_y(y, beta):
    if beta <=0: return ones/len
    m=y.max()
    z=y-m
    e=np.exp(beta*z)
    s=e.sum()
    if s==0 or not finite: uniform
    return e/s

Smooth gradient via FFT:
def _grad_fft(p, a, n, L=None):
    # p length 2n-1, a length n
    if L is None: L=_next_fft_len(n)
    P=np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    z=np.fft.irfft(P * np.conj(A), L)
    return 2.0 * z[:n].real
Need verify for n=1: p len1, a len1; L=1; z[0]=p0 a0, grad=2p0a0. Derivative of a0^2=2a0, ok.

Hard gradient for one bin:
def _grad_hard(a,k,n):
    g=np.zeros(n)
    lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
    if lo<=hi:
        idx=np.arange(lo,hi+1)
        src=k-idx
        g[idx]=2.0*a[src]
    return g
This is correct; if k can be 0..2n-2. For k small, src valid.

Internal objective:
def _normalize(a,n):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    target=np.sqrt(n)
    if s<1e-300: return np.full(n, target/n)
    a=a*target/s
    # cap to prevent huge? if max>1000? not.
    return a
def _obj_val(u,n):
    s=u.sum(); if s<1e-300 return inf
    y=_conv_fft(u,n) # but if called often, we have y
    return 2.0*n*float(y.max())/(s*s)
def _obj_from_y(y,n,u_sum=None):
    if u_sum is None: u_sum=np.sqrt(n)
    if u_sum<1e-300: return inf
    return 2.0*n*float(y.max())/(u_sum*u_sum)
In refine we normalize to target, so obj=2*n*max/(n)=2*max_y. Use _obj_norm_u(y,n): 2.0*float(y.max()) because sum^2=n. However if n small, target sqrt(n). Yes.

But if we don't normalize perfectly due clipping, still close. Use _obj_from_y with sum.

def _smooth_refine(u, n, end_time, rng, tag, beta_start=1.0, beta_end=80.0, eta=0.1, max_iter=1000000):
    u=_normalize(u,n)
    best_u=u.copy(); best_y=_conv_fft(u,n); best_obj=2.0*float(best_y.max())
    print start
    beta=beta_start
    improve_count=0; fail_count=0
    t0=time.monotonic()
    total_budget=end_time-t0
    for it in range(max_iter):
        if time.monotonic() >= end_time: break
        y=_conv_fft(u,n)  # current
        max_y=float(y.max())
        obj=2.0*max_y
        if obj < best_obj - 1e-12:
            best_obj=obj; best_u=u.copy(); improve_count=0
            if it%1000==0 print
        else:
            improve_count+=1
        if max_y <= 0.75135/2? target? maybe stop if below target? We'll keep.
        # beta schedule: use progress by wall time
        if total_budget>0:
            prog=min(1.0,(time.monotonic()-t0)/total_budget)
            beta=beta_start + (beta_end-beta_start)*prog
            # if stall, increase beta
        if improve_count>200:
            beta=min(beta*1.1, 500.0)
            improve_count=0
        p=_softmax_y(y,beta)
        g=_grad_fft(p,u,n)
        # Maybe subtract a-weighted mean to preserve sum better? Since sum constraint, subtract mean of g.
        g-=g.mean()
        mg=np.abs(g).max()
        if mg<1e-15: break
        # step size: normalize by mg to make max change ~eta*max_g? u values O(1). Use eta maybe 0.1-0.5.
        accepted=False
        for attempt in range(3):
            lr=eta*(0.5**attempt)
            v=u - lr*(g/mg)
            v=np.maximum(v,0.0)
            v=_normalize(v,n)
            yv=_conv_fft(v,n)
            objv=2.0*float(yv.max())
            if objv < obj - 1e-12:
                u=v; y=yv; obj=objv; max_y=...? accepted=True; break
            # if first attempt slight increase but maybe update best? no.
        if accepted:
            fail_count=0
            # increase eta if too small? cap 0.6
            eta=min(eta*1.02,0.7)
        else:
            fail_count+=1
            eta*=0.7
            if eta<1e-5:
               # add small random perturbation
               if rng is not None:
                  u=_normalize(u + rng.normal(0,1e-4,size=n),n)
               eta=0.05
        if fail_count>1000: break
    # final maybe hard phase inside
    return best_u,best_obj

Let's detail _apply_exact(a,c,i,newval):
    old = a[i]
    d = newval - old
    if d==0: return
    # Add 2*d * a[0:n] to c[i:i+n]
    c[i:i+n] += 2.0 * d * a[:n]
    # diagonal add d^2 at 2i
    diag=2*i
    if 0 <= diag < len(c): c[diag] += d*d
    a[i] = newval
But wait if i:i+n goes beyond c length? i max n-1, i+n-1=2n-2 max, okay. But c[i:i+n] length n. The second-order term for diagonal: For i, c[2i] currently includes old^2. Slice added 2*d*old. d^2 completes. However if there are cross terms with other index updated after, sequential handles.

After j update: uses a_i=old_i+Δ_i. Slice for j adds to k=i+j: 2Δ_j (old_i+Δ_i) => adds 2old_iΔ_j +2Δ_iΔ_j. Total 2[old_iold_j + Δ_i old_j + old_iΔ_j + Δ_iΔ_j] =2(new_i new_j). Correct. def propose_candidate(seed=0,budget_s=2400):
    start_time=time.monotonic(); deadline=start_time+budget_s-15
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # parent

    parent_n=None
    if parent is not None:
       try:

         p=np.clip(p,0,1e6); p[p~finite]=0
         if p.ndim==1 and p.size>=2 and p.sum()>0:

    primary_n = parent_n if parent_n else 3809

    lengths = [primary_n]
    other_lengths = [1024,2048,5000,8000]
    # But if primary_n small, include others.
    best_list=None; best_score=inf
    # Define _try_candidate(arr,n, tag): normalize, evaluate internal, if promising run refine? Or evaluate list.

Refinement pipeline for a start:
def _refine_candidate(a,n,rng,end_time, tag, profile='full'):
    a=_normalize_n(a,n)
    obj=_obj_conv(a,n)
    # smooth 1
    a,obj = _smooth_refine(a,n,end_time1, rng, tag+'_s1', beta_start=1,beta_end=50, eta=0.05)
    # transfer
    a,obj = _local_transfer(a,n,end_time2, rng, tag+'_t', guided=True, iters...)
    # smooth 2 high beta
    a,obj = _smooth_refine(a,n,end_time3,... beta_start=20,end_beta=300, eta=0.03)
    # transfer again
    a,obj = _local_transfer...
    # LP if n small and time
    # final evaluator
    return a,obj

def _smooth_refine(a,n,end_time,rng,tag,beta_start=1.0,beta_end=100.0,eta=0.05):
    a=_normalize_n(a,n)
    c=_conv_fft(a,n); qmax=float(c.max())/n; obj=2.0*qmax
    best_a=a.copy(); best_obj=obj
    beta=float(beta_start)
    improvements=0
    stall=0
    t0=time.monotonic()
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()<end_time:
        # compute c/q each loop? We have c from previous if no step? If step accepted, c updated. If rejected, c same. We can maintain c.
        # At start, c known. Loop:
        if time... break
        # maybe if obj > best_obj + 0.05 due jitter? reset to best?
        if obj > best_obj + 0.01:
            a=best_a.copy(); c=_conv_fft(a,n); obj=best_obj
        spread = max(1e-12, float(c.max()) - float(np.mean(c)))? c values n; use q.
        # beta update: if stall>100: beta=min(beta*1.1,1000)
        p=_softmax_q(c,n,beta) # c/n
        g=2*_grad_fft(p,a,n) # no divide n
        g -= g.mean()
        mg=np.max(np.abs(g))
        if mg<1e-15: break
        accepted=False
        for lr in (eta, 0.5*eta, 0.25*eta):
            step=lr*(g/mg)
            v=a-step
            v=np.maximum(v,0.0)
            v=_normalize_n(v,n)
            vc=_conv_fft(v,n)
            vobj=2.0*float(vc.max())/n
            if vobj < obj - 1e-12:
                a=v.copy(); c=vc; obj=vobj; accepted=True; break
        if accepted:
           if obj < best_obj - 1e-12: best_obj=obj; best_a=a.copy()
           eta=min(eta*1.05,0.2); stall=0
           if ... print
        else:
           stall+=1
           eta*=0.7
           if stall%100==0: beta=min(beta*1.2,1000)
           if eta<1e-6: break
    return best_a,best_obj
```
Need `_softmax_q(c,n,beta)`: q=c/n. But c length 2n-1, beta*q. To avoid scaling, `z = c - c.max()`; `e=np.exp(beta*z/n)`. Use n. Implement:
```
def _softmax_c(c,n,beta):
    m=c.max()
    z=(c-m)/n
    e=np.exp(beta*z)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0: uniform
    return e/s
```
Beta in q units.
Beta schedule: Use b_start/b_end over time. In function, if elapsed>0: prog=min(1,elapsed/total_budget). total_budget=end_time-t0 maybe; but if short, beta jumps. Simpler update each iteration beta += (beta_end-beta_start)/max(1,it_limit?) We don't know it. Use `beta = min(beta_end, beta + max(0.02, (beta_end-beta_start)/50000))`. If stall, multiply.
Start beta=1 for global, end=200 for primary. For high phase start=50,end=500.
Eta: if many accepts, increase; if failures reduce. The line search may be expensive; if no accept, no a change, c same. We can after 200 failures add jitter: v=a+rng.normal(0,0.01, n); if normalized objective not too bad, accept to escape? But pure gradient may stagnate. We'll allow small jitter every 500 failures if obj < best+0.01? Since best maybe current. If current=best, jitter worse; maybe not. Use jitter smaller and accept even if worse up to 0.01? Let's implement `_normalize_n` with optional cap? For n large, if sum small? If a all zeros, return ones.
```
def _normalize_n(a,n):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
    if a.size!=n: ... resize? maybe.
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1e6)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-12:
       return np.ones(n)/1.0
    return a*(n/s)
```

       val=evaluate_sequence(seq_list)

    return float('inf')

def _normalize_n_cap(a,n,cap=1000.0):
   a = max(0)
   s=sum; target=n; if s<eps ones
   a=a*target/s
   if a.max()>cap:
      # set excess? Simple iterative: a[a>cap]=cap; redistribute to others <cap
      excess=a.sum()-target
      if excess>0:
         mask=a<cap
         if mask.any(): a[mask] += excess/mask.sum()
        # possibly still >cap due mask sum low; repeat few
         for _ in range(5):
            over=a>cap; if not break; excess=a.sum()-target; if excess<=0 break; mask=~over; if not mask.any(): break; a[over]=cap; a[mask]+=excess/mask.sum()
   return a

Let's detail _smooth_refine with c maintenance:
```
def _smooth_refine(a,n,end_time,rng,tag,beta_start=1.0,beta_end=100.0,eta=0.06,print_every=1000):
    a=_normalize_n(a,n)
    c=_conv_fft(a,n)
    obj=2.0*float(c.max())/n
    best_a=a.copy(); best_obj=obj
    beta=float(beta_start)
    t0=time.monotonic(); total=max(end_time-t0,1.0)
    stall=0; it=0
    print(f"smooth {tag} start obj={obj:.6f}")
    while time.monotonic()<end_time:
       it+=1
       if it%1000==0: print
       # beta by progress
       prog=min(1.0,(time.monotonic()-t0)/total)
       beta_target=beta_start + (beta_end-beta_start)*prog
       beta=max(beta, beta_target)
       if stall>100:
           beta=min(beta*1.1, 2000.0)
           stall=0
       p=_softmax_c(c,n,beta)
       g=2.0*_grad_fft(p,a,n)
       g-=g.mean()
       mg=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
       if mg<1e-14: break
       accepted=False
       for mult in (1.0,0.5):
           lr=eta*mult
           v=a-lr*(g/mg)
           v=np.maximum(v,0.0)
           v=_normalize_n(v,n)
           vc=_conv_fft(v,n)
           vo=2.0*float(vc.max())/n
           if vo < obj - 1e-12:
               a=v; c=vc; obj=vo; accepted=True; break
       if accepted:
           if obj < best_obj - 1e-12:
               best_obj=obj; best_a=a.copy(); stall=0
               if (it//1000)%10==0: print
           eta=min(eta*1.03,0.25)
       else:
           stall+=1
           eta*=0.75
           if stall % 200 ==0:
              # small jitter around best if close?
              pass
           if eta < 1e-7: break
    # if best different, final recompute? not needed
    return best_a, best_obj
```
Potential bug: If current obj > best_obj due no, but we never set current to worse, so current=best accepted? Actually if line search rejects, current unchanged; if previous current was best. Good.
But if we added jitter not implemented, no.

Could be slow due `vc` line search for two attempts; if many failures, two conv per iter. We can reduce to one attempt if eta lower. Use one: if vo not improve, don't try half in same iter; next iter eta reduced and recomputes same? c same, g same but eta lower. That's okay but wastes recompute of p/g. We can inside loop keep g and try half without recompute, only one extra conv. Current code good.

Beta_target might decrease if beta increased by stall > target; we take max. Good.

def _local_transfer(a,n,end_time,rng,tag,temp_factor=0.02,it_limit=200000):
    a=_normalize_n(a,n)
    c=np.convolve(a,a).astype(np.float64)
    cur_max=float(c.max()); best_a=a.copy(); best_max=cur_max
    best_obj=2*cur_max/n
    t0=time.monotonic(); total=max(end_time-t0,1.0)
    print...
    cap=999.0
    for it in range(it_limit):
       if time.monotonic()>=end_time: break
       if it % 10000==0: c=np.convolve(a,a).astype(float); cur_max=float(c.max()); maybe update best? if cur_max<best_max...
       if it % 20000==0 and abs(a.sum()-n)>1e-4*n: a=_normalize_n(a,n); c=np.convolve...
       cur_max = float(c.max()) # maybe we can use variable max; but after updates, c max changes. We'll use c.max each move.
       k=int(np.argmax(c))
       if cur_max < best_max - ... update best (though current accepted may be worse; we can update best any time)
       # choose j in involved range with high a
       lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
       if hi>=lo:
           best_off=lo+int(np.argmax(a[lo:hi+1]))
           # with prob 0.7 use max, else uniform
           if rng.random()<0.7: j=best_off
           else: j=int(rng.integers(lo,hi+1))
       else: j=int(rng.integers(n))
       # choose i low among samples
       if n>1:
           samp = rng.integers(n, size=8)
           vals = a[samp]
           i = samp[int(np.argmin(vals))]
           # with prob 0.2 keep i even if high
       else: continue
       if i==j: continue
       old_i=a[i]; old_j=a[j]
       # move modes: transfer or swap
       if rng.random()<0.2:
           new_i=old_j; new_j=old_i
       else:
           frac = rng.uniform(0.02,0.25)
           delta = frac*old_j
           # also allow moving from i to j if i high? if old_i > old_j and rng random maybe swap roles
           if old_i > old_j and rng.random()<0.3:
              delta = -frac*old_i
           if abs(delta)<1e-15: continue
           new_i=old_i+delta; new_j=old_j-delta
       if new_i<0 or new_j<0 or new_i>cap or new_j>cap: continue
       # apply i then j. But if swap, ensure apply order? Apply i->new_i, j->new_j.
       # Save order for undo: applied [i,j]
       _apply_delta(a,c,i,new_i)
       _apply_delta(a,c,j,new_j)
       # after apply, a[i]=new_i,a[j]=new_j
       new_max=float(c.max())
       progress=min(1.0,(time.monotonic()-t0)/total)
       T = max(1e-6, temp_factor*(1.0-0.9*progress)*cur_max + 0.01*cur_max*progress)
       accept=False
       if new_max <= cur_max:
           accept=True
       else:
           diff=(new_max-cur_max)
           if diff < T:
              # probability exp(-diff/T)
              if rng.random() < np.exp(-diff/T): accept=True
       if accept:
           cur_max=new_max
           if cur_max < best_max:
              best_max=cur_max; best_a=a.copy()
       else:
           # undo reverse: j then i to old
           _apply_delta(a,c,j,old_j)
           _apply_delta(a,c,i,old_i)
           # due roundoff, c may not exactly; if bad, reconv occasionally
    recompute final c? Return best_a. We can compute best_obj=2*best_max/n. But best_a's c maybe not exact due incremental; final evaluate later.
    return best_a, best_obj
```

if hi-lo+1 <=32: j=lo+argmax(...)

def _make_starts(n,rng, parent=None):
   starts=[]
   if parent is not None and parent.size==n:
       p=np.clip(parent,0,1e6); if sum>0: starts.append(p.copy())
   # uniform
   starts.append(np.ones(n))
   # linear skews
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n, endpoint=True)
   for c in (-1.5,-1.0,-0.5,0.5,1.0,1.5):
       f=1.0+c*(x-0.5)
       f=np.maximum(f,0.02)
       starts.append(f)
   # sine odd (antisymmetric about 0.5): base 1 + A sin(pi(2x-1))? sin changes sign; ensure positive.
   for A in (0.5,1.0,1.5):
       f=1.0+A*np.sin(np.pi*(2*x-1))
       f=np.maximum(f,0.02)
       starts.append(f)
   # random smooth positive: Fourier
   for _ in range(4):
       M=5; coeff=rng.normal(0,0.5,size=M); f=1+sum(coeff[m]*cos(2πm x + rng phase)); clip min0.02
   # random blocks: K=10 random heights lognormal, repeat to n
   # random sparse: m=max(20,n//20); zeros; random pos; random weights exp(normal); starts.append
   return starts
```

def _normalize_n(a,n,cap=999.0):
   a=np.maximum(np.nan_to_num(a),0)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<1e-15: return np.ones(n)
   a=a*(n/s)
   for _ in range(10):
       if a.max() <= cap+1e-9: break
       over=a>cap
       excess=float(a.sum()-n)
       if excess <= 0: break
       a[over]=cap
       mask=~over
       if mask.any(): a[mask]+=excess/mask.sum()
       else: # impossible if n<=cap*number? If all capped and sum>n, cannot; reduce? set a[np.argmax]=? but sum target n and all cap gives cap*n >= n, so there is mask if some <cap or number enough. If all exactly cap and cap*n n -> sum=cap*n not n unless cap=1; but if all cap=999 and n>1 sum>n, but there is mask none only if every >=cap; set over true for >cap, some ==cap not over. mask includes ==cap? Need allow mask = a < cap; if all >=cap and sum>n, cannot reduce without making below cap? We can set all to cap and then scale down? But scale down below cap. So if no mask, a = a*(n/a.sum()). Break.
   a=np.maximum(a,0)
   s=a.sum()
   if s>0: a*=n/s
   return a
```

Interpolation function:
```
def _resample_sequence(a, n_out):
    if len(a)==n_out: return a.copy()
    x_out=np.linspace(0,n_out-1)
    x_in=np.linspace(0,len(a)-1)
    return np.interp(x_out,x_in,a)
```

def _perturb_mul(a,n,rng,sigma,width_frac=0.05):
    b=a.copy()
    w=max(2,int(n*width_frac))
    s=int(rng.integers(0,n-w+1))
    b[s:s+w]*=np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,sigma,size=w))
    return _normalize_n(b,n)
```

LP step:
```
def _lp_step(a,n):
   c=np.convolve(a,a); Mc=c.max()
   v=_lp_direction(a,n,Mc)
   if v is None: return a,c
   for t in (0.01,0.03,0.1,0.2):
       w=(1-t)*a+t*v
       w=_normalize_n(w,n)
       wc=np.convolve(w,w); wm=wc.max()
       if wm < Mc:
           return w,wc
   return a,c
```

We can compute A with rfft(a) once per iteration to get c and grad; instead of separate conv. For v line search, only need c, no grad. That saves one FFT for current. Let's do:
```
def _fft_real(x,L): return np.fft.rfft(x,L)
def _c_from_fft(A,n,L): return np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)[:2n-1]
```
In smooth:

```
def _make_starts(n,rng,parent=None):
    starts=[]
    if parent is not None and parent.size==n:
       p=np.asarray(parent,float); p=...; if p.sum()>0: starts.append(p.copy())
    starts.append(np.ones(n))
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    for cval in (-1.5,-1.0,-0.5,0.5,1.0,1.5):
       f=1.0+cval*(x-0.5); f=np.maximum(f,0.02); starts.append(f)
    for A in (0.5,1.0,1.5):
       f=1.0+A*np.sin(np.pi*(2*x-1.0)); f=np.maximum(f,0.02); starts.append(f)
    for _ in range(6):
       f=np.ones(n)
       modes=int(rng.integers(2,8))
       for m in range(1,modes+1):
           amp=float(rng.normal(0.0,0.4))
           ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
           f += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x+ph)
       f=np.maximum(f,0.02); starts.append(f)
    # blocks
    K=int(min(n, rng.integers(5,21)))
    h=np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.5,K)); h=np.maximum(h,0.05)
    b=np.repeat(h, n//K+1)[:n]; starts.append(b)
    # sparse
    m=int(max(20, min(n, n//5)))
    zeros=np.zeros(n)
    idx=rng.choice(n, size=min(m,n), replace=False)
    zeros[idx]=np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.5,size=min(m,n)))
    if zeros.sum()>0: starts.append(zeros)
    return starts
```
`n//K+1` repeat may length >n; slice.
For n small (<20), K min etc. Ensure K>0. If n=1, special? We may not search n=1. But handle: if n<2, return [ones]. Add at top if n<2.

Resample:
```
def _resample_sequence(a, n_out):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    if a.size==0: return np.ones(n_out)
    if a.size==n_out: return a.copy()
    x_out=np.linspace(0.0, n_out-1, n_out)
    x_in=np.linspace(0.0, a.size-1, a.size)
    return np.interp(x_out, x_in, a)
```

`_next_fft_len(n)`:
```
def _next_fft_len(n):
    if n <= 1: return 1
    L = 1
    need = 2*n - 1
    while L < need:
        L <<= 1
    return L
```

`_rfft`: no.

`_conv_fft(a,n,L=None)`:
```
def _conv_fft(a,n,L=None):
    if L is None: L=_next_fft_len(n)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)
    c = c[:2*n-1]
    if c.size:
       c[c < 0] = 0.0
    return c
```
In-place c[c<0]=0 maybe if c has negative small; okay.

`_conv_fft_from_A(A,n,L)`: same.

`_softmax_c(c,n,beta)`:
```
def _softmax_c(c,n,beta):
    m=float(np.max(c))
    z=(c-m)/float(n)
    if beta <= 0:
       return np.full_like(c, 1.0/c.size)
    e=np.exp(beta*z)
    s=float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       return np.full_like(c, 1.0/c.size)
    return e/s
```
If z very negative, exp 0. If beta*z > 700? z max0, min negative maybe -1; beta up to2000 => exp(-2000) underflows 0 okay. If beta huge and z range large? max0, safe.

`_grad_fft_with_A(P,A,n,L)`:

```

```

   t=np.empty(n); t[:min(a.size,n)]=a[:min]; t[min:]=0; a=t

   return np.ones(n, dtype=float)
a *= n/s
# cap loop
for _ in range(20):
   if a.size and float(a.max()) <= cap:
       break
   over = a > cap
   if not over.any(): break
   excess = float(a.sum()) - float(n)
   if excess <= 1e-12: break
   a[over] = cap
   mask = ~over
   if not mask.any():
       a = a * (n / float(a.sum()))
       break

a*=n/s

# if cap still after scaling? scaling down? okay

def _normalize_n(...):
   ... initial scale
   for _ in range(10):
      over=a>cap
      if not over.any(): break
      excess=a.sum()-n

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    deadline=t0+budget_s-20.0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent=None
    try:
       p=globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
       if p is not None:
          pa=np.asarray(p,dtype=float)
          if pa.ndim==1 and pa.size>0:
             pa=np.nan_to_num(pa, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
             pa=np.clip(pa,0.0,1e6)
             if pa.size<=15000 and float(pa.sum())>0:
                parent=pa
    except Exception:
       parent=None
    if parent is not None and 10 <= parent.size <=12000:
       primary_n=int(parent.size)
    else:

          except: sc=inf
          if np.isfinite(sc): best_score=sc; best_list=pl; best_n=parent_n
    # generate primary starts
    starts=_make_starts(primary_n,rng,parent)

    best_a=scored[0][1].copy()

    # warmup optimize best_a for e.g. 400s or 25%

    if warmup_end < t0+30: warmup_end=t0+min(60, deadline-t0-30)
    a_opt,obj_opt=_optimize_candidate(best_a,primary_n,warmup_end,rng,'main')
    # update best
    ...
    # Middle perturb loop until deadline-600
    attempt=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline-600.0:
       attempt +=1
       # if best_score very good maybe less perturb? continue
       if attempt <=8:
          sigma=0.04; wf=0.02
       elif attempt <=20:
          sigma=0.10; wf=0.05
       else:
          sigma=0.20; wf=0.10
       # sometimes noise or sparse
       r=rng.random()
       if r<0.6:
          a_p=_perturb_mul(best_a,primary_n,rng,sigma,wf)
       elif r<0.85:
          a_p=_perturb_noise(best_a,primary_n,rng,sigma)
       else:
          a_p=_perturb_sparse(best_a,primary_n,rng)
       sub_end=min(deadline-600.0, time.monotonic()+45.0)
       if sub_end - time.monotonic() < 10: break
       a_new,obj_new=_optimize_candidate(a_p,primary_n,sub_end,rng,f'rest{attempt}')
       if obj_new < best_internal - 1e-9:
          best_a=a_new; best_internal=obj_new
          # evaluate true
          lst=...
          if sc < best_score: update
       # random start every 10
       if attempt % 10 ==0:
          # pick one from scored starts maybe different
          a_rand = scored[min(attempt//10, len(scored)-1)][1].copy()
          # add noise
          a_rand=_perturb_noise(a_rand,primary_n,rng,0.1)
          sub_end=min(deadline-600.0, time.monotonic()+30.0)
          a_new,obj_new=_optimize_candidate(...)
          update if better
    # Other lengths if remaining > 200
    other_end=min(deadline-80.0, time.monotonic()+400.0)
    if other_end - time.monotonic() > 60:
       lengths=[128,256,512,1024,2048,5000]
       # add primary? no
       for m in lengths:
          if time.monotonic() >= other_end - 30: break
          # time per = min(50, (other_end-now)/3)
          tsec=min(50.0, max(20.0, (other_end-time.monotonic())/3.0))
          # start from resample best
          a_start=_resample_sequence(best_a if best_a is not None else best_a, m)
          # add slight random perturbation
          a_start=_perturb_noise(a_start,m,rng,0.05)
          a_new,obj_new=_optimize_candidate(a_start,m,t0? no absolute end=time.monotonic()+tsec, rng,f'n{m}')
          # if m<=300 LP steps maybe after optimize? _optimize didn't. We can do quick LP loop:
          if m <= 300:
             lp_end=min(other_end-30, time.monotonic()+20.0)
             while time.monotonic()<lp_end:
                a_l,c_l=_lp_step(a_new,m)
                newobj=2*max(c_l)/m
                if newobj < obj_new:
                   a_new=a_l; obj_new=newobj
                else: break
             if time.monotonic()<other_end-10:
                a_new,obj_new=_local_transfer(a_new,m,min(other_end-5,time.monotonic()+10.0),rng,f'lp{n{m}}')
          # evaluate true
          lst...
          if sc < best_score: update best_n=m; best_a=a_new; best_internal=obj
          # else if internal close, maybe resample to primary later? skip
    # Final refine
    if best_list is None:
       # fallback best_a
       if best_a is not None:
          lst=...
          sc=...
          best_list=lst; best_n=best_a.size
       else: best_list=[1.0]*100
    if time.monotonic() < deadline-10:
       a_final,obj_final=_optimize_candidate(best_a,best_n,deadline-5,rng,'final')
       evaluate true; if better update
       # If best_n != primary_n and final improved maybe okay
    # Ensure best_list finite; if not fallback
    return _finalize_list(best_list)
```

```python
"""
Hybrid optimizer for minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2
over non-negative sequences.

Main ideas:
  * Work with a normalized sequence sum(a)=n.
  * Use FFT-based convolutions and gradient of a log-sum-exp smoothed max.
  * Use accepted projected gradient steps with adaptive learning rate.
  * Use exact local transfers/SA moves on the full convolution array.
  * For small lengths, optionally use a linear-programming Frank-Wolfe direction.
  * Multi-start / restart exploration over several candidate lengths.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def _normalize_n(a, n, cap=999.0):
    """Project to non-negative, sum n, and coordinates bounded by cap."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    if a.size != n:
        t = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(a.size, n)
        if m > 0:
            t[:m] = a[:m]
        a = t
    else:
        a = a.copy()

    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)

    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    a *= float(n) / s

    for _ in range(20):
        if a.size == 0:
            break
        mx = float(a.max())
        if mx <= cap:
            break
        over = a > cap
        if not np.any(over):
            break
        excess = float(a.sum()) - float(n)
        if excess <= 1e-12:
            break
        a[over] = cap
        mask = ~over
        if not np.any(mask):
            s = float(a.sum())
            if s > 0:
                a *= float(n) / s
            break
        a[mask] += excess / float(np.count_nonzero(mask))

    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    np.minimum(a, cap, out=a)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a *= float(n) / s
    return a


def _next_fft_len(n):
    if n <= 1:
        return 1
    need = 2 * n - 1
    L = 1
    while L < need:
        L <<= 1
    return L


def _conv_from_fft(A, n, L):
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)
    c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def _conv_fft(a, n, L=None):
    if L is None:
        L = _next_fft_len(n)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    return _conv_from_fft(A, n, L)


def _softmax_c(c, n, beta):
    """Softmax of beta * c / n, i.e. softmax in normalized convolution units."""
    csize = c.size
    if csize == 0:
        return c.copy()
    if beta <= 0.0:
        return np.full(csize, 1.0 / csize, dtype=np.float64)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    z = (c - m) / float(n)
    e = np.exp(beta * z)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(csize, 1.0 / csize, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _smooth_refine(a, n, end_time, rng, tag, beta_start=1.0, beta_end=120.0, eta=0.05):
    """
    Accepted-projected-gradient refinement of the normalized max coefficient.
    Objective internally is 2*max(c)/n when sum(a)=n.
    """
    a = _normalize_n(a, n)
    if n <= 1:
        return a, 2.0

    L = _next_fft_len(n)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = _conv_from_fft(A, n, L)
    obj = 2.0 * float(c.max()) / float(n)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_obj = obj
    beta = float(beta_start)
    stall = 0
    it = 0
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = max(float(end_time - t0), 1.0)

    while time.monotonic() < end_time:
        it += 1

        prog = min(1.0, (time.monotonic() - t0) / total)
        target_beta = beta_start + (beta_end - beta_start) * prog
        if beta < target_beta:
            beta = float(target_beta)
        if stall > 100:
            beta = min(float(beta) * 1.12, 3000.0)
            stall = 0

        p = _softmax_c(c, n, beta)
        P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
        g = np.fft.irfft(P * np.conj(A), L)
        g = 2.0 * np.asarray(g[:n], dtype=np.float64)
        g -= float(g.mean())
        mg = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if mg < 1e-15:
            break

        accepted = False
        for mult in (1.0, 0.5):
            lr = eta * mult
            v = a - lr * (g / mg)
            v = np.maximum(v, 0.0)
            v = _normalize_n(v, n)
            B = np.fft.rfft(v, L)
            cv = _conv_from_fft(B, n, L)
            ov = 2.0 * float(cv.max()) / float(n)
            if ov < obj - 1e-12:
                a = v
                A = B
                c = cv
                obj = ov
                accepted = True
                break

        if accepted:
            if obj < best_obj - 1e-12:
                best_obj = obj
                best_a = a.copy()
                stall = 0
                if (it // 1000) % 10 == 0:
                    print(f"  [smooth {tag}] it={it} obj={obj:.6f} beta={beta:.2f} eta={eta:.4f}")
            eta = min(eta * 1.03, 0.25)
        else:
            stall += 1
            eta *= 0.75
            if eta < 1e-8:
                break

    return best_a, best_obj


def _apply_delta(a, c, i, newval):
    """Exact incremental update of full self-convolution c when a[i] changes."""
    i = int(i)
    old = float(a[i])
    d = float(newval) - old
    if abs(d) < 1e-15:
        return
    n = a.size
    c[i:i + n] += 2.0 * d * a
    diag = 2 * i
    if 0 <= diag < c.size:
        c[diag] += d * d
    a[i] = float(newval)


def _local_transfer(a, n, end_time, rng, tag, temp_factor=0.03):
    """
    Local simulated-annealing mass-transfer / swap moves with exact convolution updates.
    """
    if n <= 1:
        return a, 2.0

    a = _normalize_n(a, n)
    c = np.asarray(np.convolve(a, a), dtype=np.float64)
    cur_max = float(c.max())

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_max = cur_max
    best_obj = 2.0 * cur_max / float(n)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = max(float(end_time - t0), 1.0)
    cap = 999.0

    for it in range(10000000):
        if time.monotonic() >= end_time:
            break

        if it % 10000 == 0:
            a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
            s = float(a.sum())
            if s <= 0:
                a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            else:
                a *= float(n) / s
            c = np.asarray(np.convolve(a, a), dtype=np.float64)
            cur_max = float(c.max())

        cur_max = float(c.max())
        if cur_max < best_max:
            best_max = cur_max
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_obj = 2.0 * best_max / float(n)

        if best_max > 0.0 and cur_max > 1.12 * best_max:
            a = best_a.copy()
            c = np.asarray(np.convolve(a, a), dtype=np.float64)
            cur_max = float(c.max())
            continue

        k = int(np.argmax(c))
        lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
        hi = min(n - 1, k)
        length = hi - lo + 1

        if length <= 0:
            j = int(rng.integers(0, n))
        elif length <= 64:
            j = lo + int(np.argmax(a[lo:hi + 1]))
        else:
            sj = min(32, length)
            offs = rng.integers(lo, hi + 1, size=sj)
            j = int(offs[int(np.argmax(a[offs]))])

        si = min(16, n)
        samp_i = rng.integers(0, n, size=si)
        i = int(samp_i[int(np.argmin(a[samp_i]))])

        if i == j:
            continue

        old_i = float(a[i])
        old_j = float(a[j])
        if old_i <= 1e-12 and old_j <= 1e-12:
            continue

        rmode = rng.random()
        if rmode < 0.18:
            # swap
            new_i = old_j
            new_j = old_i
        else:
            frac = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.28))
            # transfer from j to i by default
            delta = frac * old_j
            # with some probability, transfer from the larger to the smaller
            if old_i > old_j and rng.random() < 0.35:
                delta = -frac * old_i
            if abs(delta) < 1e-15:
                continue
            new_i = old_i + delta
            new_j = old_j - delta

        if new_i < -1e-9 or new_j < -1e-9 or new_i > cap or new_j > cap:
            continue

        _apply_delta(a, c, i, new_i)
        _apply_delta(a, c, j, new_j)

        new_max = float(c.max())
        progress = min(1.0, (time.monotonic() - t0) / total)
        T = max(1e-9, (0.5 * temp_factor * (1.0 - 0.96 * progress) + 0.002 * progress) * max(1.0, cur_max))

        accept = False
        if new_max <= cur_max:
            accept = True
        else:
            diff = new_max - cur_max
            x = diff / T
            if x < 50.0:
                if rng.random() < float(np.exp(-x)):
                    accept = True

        if accept:
            cur_max = new_max
            if cur_max < best_max:
                best_max = cur_max
                best_a = a.copy()
                best_obj = 2.0 * best_max / float(n)
                if (it // 2000) % 10 == 0:
                    print(f"  [transfer {tag}] it={it} obj={best_obj:.6f}")
        else:
            # undo in reverse order
            _apply_delta(a, c, j, old_j)
            _apply_delta(a, c, i, old_i)

    return best_a, best_obj


def _perturb_mul(a, n, rng, sigma, width_frac=0.05):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if n <= 1:
        return a
    w = max(1, int(n * width_frac))
    if w >= n:
        s = 0
        w = n
    else:
        s = int(rng.integers(0, n - w + 1))
    a[s:s + w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=w))
    return _normalize_n(a, n)


def _perturb_noise(a, n, rng, sigma=0.08):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a = a + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=n)
    return _normalize_n(a, n)


def _perturb_sparse(a, n, rng, keep_frac=0.2):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    m = int(max(1, min(n, max(5, int(n * keep_frac)))))
    if m >= n:
        return _normalize_n(a, n)
    idx = rng.choice(n, size=m, replace=False)
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b[idx] = a[idx]
    return _normalize_n(b, n)


def _make_starts(n, rng, parent=None):
    if n <= 1:
        return [np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)]

    starts = []

    if parent is not None and parent.size == n:
        p = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
        p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
        if float(p.sum()) > 0:
            starts.append(p)

    starts.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    # Odd linear / cubic distortions; they reduce the central asymmetric pairing.
    for cval in (-1.5, -1.0, -0.5, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5):
        f = 1.0 + cval * (x - 0.5)
        f = np.maximum(f, 0.02)
        starts.append(f.astype(np.float64))

    for a1 in (0.35, 0.7, 1.0, 1.4):
        f = 1.0 + a1 * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 3
        f = np.maximum(f, 0.02)
        starts.append(f.astype(np.float64))

    # Smooth odd sine wave starts.
    for A in (0.4, 0.8, 1.2, 1.6):
        f = 1.0 + A * np.sin(np.pi * (2.0 * x - 1.0))
        f = np.maximum(f, 0.02)
        starts.append(f.astype(np.float64))

    # Random positive smooth Fourier starts.
    for _ in range(6):
        f = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        modes = int(rng.integers(2, 9))
        for mm in range(1, modes + 1):
            amp = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.35))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            f += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * mm * x + ph)
        f = np.maximum(f, 0.02)
        starts.append(f)

    # Random block step start.
    K = int(min(n, max(5, rng.integers(8, 25))))
    h = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.6, size=K))
    h = np.maximum(h, 0.05)
    block = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for bidx in range(K):
        s = int(bidx * n / K)
        e = int((bidx + 1) * n / K)
        if e > s:
            block[s:e] = h[bidx]
    starts.append(block)

    # Sparse random start.
    m = int(max(1, min(n, max(5, n // 5))))
    zeros = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    idx = rng.choice(n, size=m, replace=False)
    zeros[idx] = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.6, size=m))
    if float(zeros.sum()) > 0:
        starts.append(zeros)

    return starts


def _resample_sequence(a, n_out):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n_out, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == n_out:
        return a.copy()
    x_in = np.linspace(0.0, a.size - 1, a.size)
    x_out = np.linspace(0.0, n_out - 1, n_out)
    return np.interp(x_out, x_in, a).astype(np.float64)


def _optimize_candidate(a, n, end_time, rng, tag):
    """Run a multi-phase local optimization on one candidate."""
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    a = _normalize_n(a, n)
    if n <= 1:
        return a, 2.0

    total = max(float(end_time - t0), 1.0)

    if total > 80.0:
        e1 = min(float(end_time), t0 + 0.35 * total)
        a, obj = _smooth_refine(a, n, e1, rng, tag + ":s1",
                                beta_start=1.0, beta_end=120.0, eta=0.05)

        e2 = min(float(end_time), max(time.monotonic() + 1.0, t0 + 0.60 * total))
        if time.monotonic() < e2:
            a, obj = _local_transfer(a, n, e2, rng, tag + ":t1")

        e3 = min(float(end_time), max(time.monotonic() + 1.0, t0 + 0.85 * total))
        if time.monotonic() < e3:
            a, obj = _smooth_refine(a, n, e3, rng, tag + ":s2",
                                    beta_start=30.0, beta_end=600.0, eta=0.04)

        a, obj = _local_transfer(a, n, float(end_time), rng, tag + ":t2")
    else:
        e1 = min(float(end_time), t0 + 0.55 * total)
        a, obj = _smooth_refine(a, n, e1, rng, tag + ":ss",
                                beta_start=6.0, beta_end=300.0, eta=0.08)
        a, obj = _local_transfer(a, n, float(end_time), rng, tag + ":tt")

    # Re-evaluate consistently with FFT normalization.
    c = _conv_fft(a, n)
    obj = 2.0 * float(c.max()) / float(n)
    return a, obj


def _lp_direction(a, n, Mc):
    """
    Solve, for small n,
        maximize sum(v) s.t. conv(a,v)_k <= Mc, 0 <= v <= cap.
    Since a itself is feasible, optimum sum(v) >= n.
    """
    if n < 2 or n > 300:
        return None
    try:
        from scipy.optimize import linprog
        from scipy.sparse import coo_matrix
    except Exception:
        return None

    cap = 999.0
    rows_list = []
    for i in range(n):
        rows_list.append(np.arange(i, i + n, dtype=np.int32))
    rows_flat = np.concatenate(rows_list)
    cols_flat = np.tile(np.arange(n, dtype=np.int32), n)
    data = np.repeat(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64), n)

    A = coo_matrix((data, (rows_flat, cols_flat)), shape=(2 * n - 1, n)).tocsc()
    b = np.full(2 * n - 1, float(Mc), dtype=np.float64)

    try:
        res = linprog(
            c=-np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64),
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b,
            bounds=[(0.0, cap)] * n,
            method="highs",
            options={"presolve": True}
        )
    except Exception:
        return None

    if not getattr(res, "success", False):
        return None

    v = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.maximum(np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=cap, neginf=0.0), 0.0)
    np.minimum(v, cap, out=v)
    s = float(v.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    v *= float(n) / s

    # numerical feasibility check
    av = np.convolve(a, v)
    if float(av.max()) > float(Mc) * 1.02 + 1e-7:
        return None

    return v


def _lp_step(a, n):
    """Take one LP/Frank-Wolfe style step if it improves the exact max."""
    if n <= 1:
        return a, np.asarray(np.convolve(a, a), dtype=np.float64)

    c = np.asarray(np.convolve(a, a), dtype=np.float64)
    Mc = float(c.max())
    v = _lp_direction(a, n, Mc)
    if v is None:
        return a, c

    for tt in (0.01, 0.03, 0.08, 0.18):
        w = (1.0 - tt) * a + tt * v
        w = _normalize_n(w, n)
        cw = np.asarray(np.convolve(w, w), dtype=np.float64)
        if float(cw.max()) < Mc - 1e-12:
            return w, cw

    return a, c


def _finalize_list(seq):
    out = []
    for x in seq:
        y = float(x)
        if not np.isfinite(y):
            y = 0.0
        if y < 0.0:
            y = 0.0
        if y > 1000.0:
            y = 1000.0
        out.append(y)
    if not out:
        out = [1.0]
    return out


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    """
    Main search function returning a Python list of non-negative heights.
    """
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t_start + float(budget_s) - 25.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = None
    try:
        p = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if p is not None:
            pa = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
            if pa.ndim == 1 and pa.size > 0:
                pa = np.nan_to_num(pa, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
                pa = np.clip(pa, 0.0, 1e6)
                if 5 <= pa.size <= 12000 and float(pa.sum()) > 0.0:
                    parent = pa
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        primary_n = int(parent.size)
    else:
        primary_n = 3809

    print(f"primary_n={primary_n} budget={budget_s}")

    best_score = float("inf")
    best_list = None
    best_n = primary_n
    best_internal = float("inf")
    best_a = None

    # Evaluate parent if available.
    if parent is not None:
        try:
            pl = [float(x) for x in np.clip(parent, 0.0, 1000.0)]
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(pl))
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                best_score = sc
                best_list = pl
                best_n = int(parent.size)
                print(f"parent score={sc:.6f} n={best_n}")
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Build primary starts.
    starts = _make_starts(primary_n, rng, parent)
    scored = []
    for st in starts:
        if len(scored) >= 12:
            break
        a = _normalize_n(st, primary_n)
        c = _conv_fft(a, primary_n)
        obj = 2.0 * float(c.max()) / float(primary_n)
        scored.append((obj, a))

    # Include normalized parent directly if same length.
    if parent is not None and parent.size == primary_n:
        a = _normalize_n(parent, primary_n)
        c = _conv_fft(a, primary_n)
        obj = 2.0 * float(c.max()) / float(primary_n)
        scored.append((obj, a))

    scored.sort()

    if len(scored) > 0:
        best_a = np.asarray(scored[0][1], dtype=np.float64).copy()
        best_internal = float(scored[0][0])
        print(f"best start internal obj={best_internal:.6f}")
    else:
        best_a = np.ones(primary_n, dtype=np.float64)
        best_internal = 2.0

    # Warm-up against the best primary start.
    warmup_budget = 0.36 * max(deadline - t_start, 1.0)
    warmup_end = min(deadline - 700.0, t_start + warmup_budget)
    if warmup_end < t_start + 30.0:
        warmup_end = t_start + max(10.0, min(60.0, deadline - t_start - 10.0))

    if time.monotonic() < warmup_end:
        a_opt, obj_opt = _optimize_candidate(best_a, primary_n, warmup_end, rng, "main")
        if obj_opt < best_internal:
            best_a = a_opt
            best_internal = obj_opt
        else:
            best_a = np.asarray(a_opt, dtype=np.float64).copy()

        try:
            lst = [float(x) for x in best_a]
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_list = lst
                best_n = primary_n
                print(f"after main warmup evaluated={sc:.6f}")
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Primary restart / perturbation loop.
    attempt = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 650.0:
        attempt += 1

        if attempt <= 6:
            sigma = 0.035
            wf = 0.02
        elif attempt <= 18:
            sigma = 0.08
            wf = 0.05
        elif attempt <= 40:
            sigma = 0.16
            wf = 0.10
        else:
            sigma = 0.28
            wf = 0.18

        r = rng.random()
        if r < 0.55:
            a_p = _perturb_mul(best_a, primary_n, rng, sigma, wf)
        elif r < 0.80:
            a_p = _perturb_noise(best_a, primary_n, rng, sigma * 0.7)
        else:
            a_p = _perturb_sparse(best_a, primary_n, rng, keep_frac=0.25)

        sub_end = min(deadline - 650.0, time.monotonic() + 45.0)
        if sub_end - time.monotonic() < 15.0:
            break

        a_new, obj_new = _optimize_candidate(a_p, primary_n, sub_end, rng, f"r{attempt}")
        if obj_new < best_internal - 1e-9:
            best_a = a_new
            best_internal = obj_new
            try:
                lst = [float(x) for x in best_a]
                sc = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_list = lst
                    best_n = primary_n
                    print(f"restart {attempt}: evaluated={sc:.6f}")
            except Exception:
                pass

        # Periodic fresh primary start.
        if attempt % 12 == 0 and len(scored) > 0:
            idx = min(attempt // 12, len(scored) - 1)
            a_rand = np.asarray(scored[idx][1], dtype=np.float64).copy()
            a_rand = _perturb_noise(a_rand, primary_n, rng, 0.08)
            sub_end2 = min(deadline - 650.0, time.monotonic() + 28.0)
            if sub_end2 - time.monotonic() > 12.0:
                a_new2, obj_new2 = _optimize_candidate(a_rand, primary_n, sub_end2, rng, f"fresh{attempt}")
                if obj_new2 < best_internal - 1e-9:
                    best_a = a_new2
                    best_internal = obj_new2
                    try:
                        lst = [float(x) for x in best_a]
                        sc = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
                        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                            best_score = sc
                            best_list = lst
                            best_n = primary_n
                            print(f"fresh {attempt}: evaluated={sc:.6f}")
                    except Exception:
                        pass

    # Explore a few other lengths; if the true objective improves, keep it.
    other_end = min(deadline - 90.0, time.monotonic() + 420.0)
    if other_end - time.monotonic() > 80.0:
        lengths = [128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048, 5000]
        keep = []
        for m in lengths:
            if m == primary_n:
                continue
            if m < 64 or m > 8000:
                continue
            keep.append(m)

        for m in keep:
            if time.monotonic() > other_end - 35.0:
                break

            if best_a is not None and best_a.size > 1:
                a_start = _resample_sequence(best_a, m)
            else:
                a_start = _make_starts(m, rng, None)[0]
            a_start = _perturb_noise(a_start, m, rng, 0.05)

            t_sec = min(55.0, max(22.0, (other_end - time.monotonic()) / 3.0))
            end_now = time.monotonic() + t SEC if False else time.monotonic() + t_sec
            a_new, obj_new = _optimize_candidate(a_start, m, end_now, rng, f"len{m}")

            # For small lengths, add LP steps / extra local transfer.
            if m <= 300:
                lp_end = min(other_end - 30.0, time.monotonic() + 18.0)
                last_lp_obj = None
                while time.monotonic() < lp_end:
                    a_lp, c_lp = _lp_step(a_new, m)
                    obj_lp = 2.0 * float(np.max(c_lp)) / float(m)
                    if obj_lp < obj_new - 1e-9:
                        a_new = a_lp
                        obj_new = obj_lp
                        if last_lp_obj is not None and abs(obj_lp - last_lp_obj) < 1e-9:
                            break
                        last_lp_obj = obj_lp
                    else:
                        a_new = _perturb_noise(a_new, m, rng, 0.05)
                        c_tmp = _conv_fft(a_new, m)
                        obj_new = 2.0 * float(c_tmp.max()) / float(m)

                if time.monotonic() < other_end - 10.0:
                    a_new, obj_new = _local_transfer(
                        a_new, m, min(other_end - 5.0, time.monotonic() + 12.0),
                        rng, f"len{m}_lp_t"
                    )

            if obj_new < best_internal + 0.2:
                try:
                    lst = [float(x) for x in a_new]
                    sc = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                        best_score = sc
                        best_list = lst
                        best_n = int(m)
                        best_a = np.asarray(a_new, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                        best_internal = obj_new
                        print(f"length {m} evaluated={sc:.6f}")
                except Exception:
                    pass
            else:
                best_a_for_other = a_new
                # even if not globally best yet, use it later if best_a is None
                if best_a is None:
                    best_a = a_new
                    best_internal = obj_new

    # Final refinement of the currently best sequence.
    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.ones(primary_n, dtype=np.float64)
        best_internal = 2.0

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 8.0:
        final_n = int(best_a.size)
        a_final, obj_final = _optimize_candidate(best_a, final_n, deadline - 5.0, rng, "final")
        if obj_final < best_internal:
            best_a = a_final
            best_internal = obj_final
        try:
            lst = [float(x) for x in best_a]
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_list = lst
                best_n = final_n
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Ensure a finite returned list.
    if best_list is None or len(best_list) == 0:
        try:
            best_list = [float(x) for x in best_a]
        except Exception:
            best_list = [1.0] * 100

    try:
        final_check = float(evaluate_sequence(best_list))
    except Exception:
        final_check = float("inf")

    if not np.isfinite(final_check):
        # If all we have is unsafely scored, return a simple valid sequence.
        try:
            if parent is not None:
                best_list = [float(x) for x in np.clip(parent, 0.0, 1000.0)]
                final_check = float(evaluate_sequence(best_list))
            else:
                best_list = [1.0] * 1000
                final_check = float(evaluate_sequence(best_list))
        except Exception:
            best_list = [1.0] * 1000
            final_check = float("inf")

    print(f"FINAL internal_best={best_internal:.6f} final_eval={final_check:.6f} n={len(best_list)}")
    return _finalize_list(best_list)
```
